import MethodEndpoint from "@theme/ApiExplorer/MethodEndpoint";
import ParamsDetails from "@theme/ParamsDetails";

<Heading
  as={"h1"}
  className={"openapi__heading"}
  children={"查询自动化详情"}
>
</Heading>

<MethodEndpoint
  method={"get"}
  path={"/automations/{automationId}"}
  context={"endpoint"}
>
  
</MethodEndpoint>



查询自动化详情，支持通过自动化 ID 查询自动化详情。

按返回的 configFormat 判断 JSON_AUTOMATION / FLOW_CANONICAL；列表分类不等于更新资格。

自动化不存在或 ID 格式无效时，返回 HTTP 400。


<Heading
  id={"request"}
  as={"h2"}
  className={"openapi-tabs__heading"}
  children={"Request"}
>
</Heading>

<ParamsDetails
  parameters={[{"name":"Lang","in":"header","required":false,"schema":{"type":"string","example":"{{Lang}}"},"description":"语言。使用 `zh-CN` 表示中文，使用 `en-US` 表示英文。"},{"in":"path","name":"automationId","required":true,"schema":{"type":"string"},"description":"自动化ID"},{"in":"query","name":"includeMetadata","required":false,"schema":{"type":"boolean","default":false},"description":"是否回显对象用户 metadata。"}]}
>
  
</ParamsDetails>

<RequestSchema
  title={"Body"}
  body={undefined}
>
  
</RequestSchema>

<StatusCodes
  id={undefined}
  label={undefined}
  responses={{"200":{"description":"请求发起成功","content":{"application/json":{"schema":{"allOf":[{"type":"object","x-business-success-condition":"code == 0","required":["type","version","msgId","code"],"properties":{"type":{"type":"string","description":"消息类型"},"version":{"type":"string","description":"接口版本","example":"v1"},"msgId":{"type":"string","description":"消息 ID"},"code":{"type":"integer","description":"业务状态码；仅 0 表示业务成功，HTTP 200 下的非 0 值仍表示业务失败"},"message":{"type":"string","description":"提示信息"}},"title":"BaseResponse"},{"type":"object","properties":{"data":{"nullable":true,"allOf":[{"type":"object","required":["automationId","name","enabled","definitionType","createTime","spaces"],"properties":{"automationId":{"type":"string","description":"自动化
